## Embedded Files
cf7cf68c6d6d64626a7b99e719c4321087cdf45d: $$\bar{X_{n}}=\frac{X_{1}+X_{2}+\dots +X_{n}}{n}$$

2f86e2852ad18b04b2d2a8eed1e389a483a1ee47: $$E[\bar{X_{n}}]=E[\frac{X_{1}+X_{2}+\dots +X_{n}}{n}]=\mu$$

10fa62895a85439c23ffb127b59cf6786654676d: $$P(|\bar{X_n}-\mu| \geq \epsilon) \leq \frac{\sigma^2}{n \epsilon^2} \quad \forall \epsilon >0$$

75e6858427047b50c9fa4421f6b3742127d79c94: $$\lim_{ n \to \infty }P(|\bar{X_n}-\mu| \geq \epsilon) =0$$

7c1abd19d62a1e0653f1671c35dff758402aba57: $$\text{Var}(\bar{X}_n)=Var(\frac{X_{1}+X_{2}+\dots +X_{n}}{n})=\frac{\sigma^2}{n}$$

ba3cf3c9bb93b0f8f8fe65b855e6b79882b88e9d: $$P\left(\lim_{n \to \infty} \bar{X_n} = \mu \right)=1$$

d676933c58d384e62212b9766500d07049157c25: $$P\left(\lim_{n \to \infty} \frac{F_n}{n} \right)=P(E)$$

7df17d8ee9651517ed30c0bb2a7faee903342886: $$\lim_{ n \to \infty }P\left( a \le \frac{W_{n}}{\sigma\sqrt{  n}} \le b \right)=P(a \le Z \le b) \quad Z \sim N(0,1)$$

87e1628c9ba8abaa754ce1afca7cee24cfb60608: $$W_n=\sum^n_{i=1} ( X_i - \mu)$$
